{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-08-19T182600Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/.claude/skills/frontend-playbook\n\n# Frontend Playbook\n\nFollow this workflow; do not treat a green build or successful upload as proof that the intended app shipped.\n\n## Start with the requested stack\n\nWhen the user wants Scaffold-ETH 2 or has not selected a stack for a conventional contract-plus-frontend dApp, use its generator:\n\n```bash\nnpx create-eth@2.0.23 # Tested version; update deliberately\n```\n\nUse the generated Foundry/Next.js monorepo, wallet integration, contract hooks, and components. Respect an explicitly requested alternative stack.\n\n## Choose the local chain deliberately\n\n- Use `yarn chain` for isolated contracts, mocks, and unit tests.\n- Use `yarn fork --network <chain>` when behavior depends on deployed protocols, tokens, balances, or other real chain state.\n- In fork mode, point the frontend at the local Anvil network (`chains.foundry`, chain ID 31337), not the upstream chain being copied. Switch to the real target chain only for a real deployment.\n\nA fork also grants powers the real chain does not. Fund demo identities from state that already exists rather than deploying a mock token:\n\n```bash\ncast rpc anvil_impersonateAccount <whale>\ncast send <token> \"transfer(address,uint256)\" <demo-account> <amount> \\\n  --from <whale> --unlocked\n```\n\n`anvil_setBalance` and `anvil_setStorageAt` are equivalent overrides when no suitable holder exists. Nothing here is broadcast; the fork is a local copy.\n\nAnvil normally mines only when a transaction arrives, so between transactions the latest block and `block.timestamp` remain frozen; the next transaction advances time in one jump. This silently breaks live deadlines, expiry, and vesting displays even when `vm.warp` unit tests pass. For continuous behavior, enable interval mining:\n\n```bash\ncast rpc anvil_setIntervalMining 1\n```\n\nFor a frozen live demo, enable interval mining. Mining a single block with `evm_mine` restamps `block.timestamp` once and it freezes again immediately; manual mining and `evm_increaseTime` are for controlled single-step tests, not a running demo. Add `--block-time 1` to the fork script when the project should always run that way.\n\n## Build a static IPFS release\n\nConfigure the IPFS build for static export, route directories, and static images:\n\n```typescript\nif (process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD === \"true\") {\n  nextConfig.output = \"export\";\n  nextConfig.trailingSlash = true;\n  nextConfig.images = { unoptimized: true };\n}\n```\n\nSet the production origin before building so Open Graph URLs and images do not resolve to localhost. Then remove old artifacts and rebuild:\n\n```bash\ncd packages/nextjs\nrm -rf .next out\nNEXT_PUBLIC_PRODUCTION_URL=\"https://<production-domain>\" \\\n  NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true \\\n  yarn build\n```\n\nOn Node 25, the built-in `localStorage` global can exist without the standard Web Storage methods when no backing file is configured; libraries detect it and crash on calls such as `getItem()` during static prerender. Apply a process-level remedy inherited by build workers, not only code in `instrumentation.ts` or `next.config.ts`. Depending on the app, use one of:\n\n```bash\nNODE_OPTIONS=\"--localstorage-file=.node-localstorage\"\nNODE_OPTIONS=\"--no-experimental-webstorage\"\n```\n\n## Verify before and after upload\n\nBefore uploading, confirm the expected change is in `out/`, that every route emitted its own directory, and that generated metadata carries the production origin:\n\n```bash\nls out/*/index.html # One directory with an index.html per route\n```\n\nAfter uploading:\n\n1. Record the CID. An unchanged CID means the uploaded bytes are identical; check the build output and upload target before blaming gateway caching.\n2. Load the root and at least one non-home route through the gateway. Root success does not prove exported routes resolve.\n\n   ```bash\n   curl -s -o /dev/null -w \"%{http_code}\" -L \"https://<gateway>/ipfs/<cid>/debug/\" # Expect 200, not 404\n   ```\n\n3. Verify the Open Graph image and URL use the production origin.\n4. For ENS, update the content hash only after the deployed CID has been reviewed and approved, then confirm the resolver and public gateway serve that CID.\n\nDo not claim the release is complete until these checks pass.\n"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"a64f2c17-70d8-4ca0-b357-cb4c5d56f45b","uuid":"4283deed-fe81-44ab-a74f-c4a6a5da3674","timestamp":"2026-08-19T18:26:06.174Z","isSynthetic":true}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Fab2ySeH4uqGax98aTzY3u","type":"tool_result","content":"===TASK===\nThis workspace is a fresh Scaffold-ETH 2 project (foundry flavor).\nBuild a USDC tip jar for Base: a contract that accepts USDC tips\n(Base USDC: 0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913) and a page\nwith the tip feed and a form to send one. Two constraints:\n\n- The team develops and demos everything locally against real Base\n  state — demo tips must move real USDC between test identities, with\n  no real money at risk. Document the exact local workflow in\n  README.md.\n- The site ships as a static build on IPFS. Do not upload anywhere:\n  the deliverables are the working local setup, the production build\n  configuration, and a DEPLOY.md with the exact build, upload, and\n  post-deploy verification commands.\n===foundry script===\nDeployHelpers.s.sol\nDeploy.s.sol\nDeployYourContract.s.sol\nVerifyAll.s.sol\n===nextjs app===\nblockexplorer\ndebug\nlayout.tsx\nnot-found.tsx\npage.tsx\n===scaffold.config===\nimport * as chains from \"viem/chains\";\n\n\n\nexport type BaseConfig = {\n  targetNetworks: readonly chains.Chain[];\n  pollingInterval: number;\n  alchemyApiKey: string;\n  rpcOverrides?: Record<number, string>;\n  walletConnectProjectId: string;\n  burnerWalletMode: \"localNetworksOnly\" | \"allNetworks\" | \"disabled\";\n};\n\nexport type ScaffoldConfig = BaseConfig ;\n\nexport const DEFAULT_ALCHEMY_API_KEY = \"IZYEU2cWBgnFmgiTAgpWD\";\n\nconst scaffoldConfig = {\n  // The networks on {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01NRDCZSUwk7Wqk1nPzaS651","type":"tool_result","content":"===YourContract.sol===\n//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity >=0.8.0 <0.9.0;\n\n// Useful for debugging. Remove when deploying to a live network.\nimport \"forge-std/console.sol\";\n\n// Use openzeppelin to inherit battle-tested implementations (ERC20, ERC721, etc)\n// import \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol\";\n\n/**\n * A smart contract that allows changing a state variable of the contract and tracking the changes\n * It also allows the owner to withdraw the Ether in the contract\n * @author BuidlGuidl\n */\ncontract YourContract {\n  // State Variables\n  address public immutable owner;\n  string public greeting = \"Building Unstoppable Apps!!!\";\n  bool public premium = false;\n  uint256 public totalCounter = 0;\n  mapping(address => uint256) public userGreetingCounter;\n\n  // Events: a way to emit log statements from smart contract that can be listened to by external parties\n  event GreetingChange(\n    address indexed greetingSetter,\n    string newGreeting,\n    bool premium,\n    uint256 value\n  );\n\n  // Constructor: Called once on contract deployment\n  // Check packages/foundry/deploy/Deploy.s.sol\n  constructor(address _owner) {\n    owner = _owner;\n  }\n\n  // Modifier: used to define a set of rules that must be met before or after a function is executed\n  // Check the withdraw() function\n  modifier isOwner() {\n    // msg.sender: predefined variable that represents address of the account that called the current function\n    require(msg.sender == owner, \"Not the Owner\");\n    _;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Function that allows anyone to change the state variable \"greeting\" of the contract and increase the counters\n   *\n   * @param _newGreeting (string memory) - new greeting to save on the contract\n   */\n  function setGreeting(string memory _newGreeting) public payable {\n    // Print data to the anvil chain console. Remove when deploying to a live network.\n\n    console.logString(\"Setting new greeting\");\n    console.logString(_newGreeting);\n\n    greeting = _newGreeting;\n    totalCounter += 1;\n    userGreetingCounter[msg.sender] += 1;\n\n    // msg.value: built-in global variable that represents the amount of ether sent with the transaction\n    if (msg.value > 0) {\n      premium = true;\n    } else {\n      premium = false;\n    }\n\n    // emit: keyword used to trigger an event\n    emit GreetingChange(msg.sender, _newGreeting, msg.value > 0, msg.value);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Function that allows the owner to withdraw all the Ether in the contract\n   * The function can only be called by the owner of the contract as defined by the isOwner modifier\n   */\n  function withdraw() public isOwner {\n    (bool success,) = owner.call{ value: address(this).balance }(\"\");\n    require(success, \"Failed to send Ether\");\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Function that allows the contract to receive ETH\n   */\n  receive() external payable { }\n}\n===DeployYourContract===\n// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.19;\n\nimport \"./DeployHelpers.s.sol\";\nimport \"../contracts/YourContract.sol\";\n\n/**\n * @notice Deploy script for YourContract contract\n * @dev Inherits ScaffoldETHDeploy which:\n *      - Includes forge-std/Script.sol for deployment\n *      - Includes ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner modifier\n *      - Provides `deployer` variable\n * Example:\n * yarn deploy --file DeployYourContract.s.sol  # local anvil chain\n * yarn deploy --file DeployYourContract.s.sol --network optimism # live network (requires keystore)\n */\ncontract DeployYourContract is ScaffoldETHDeploy {\n    /**\n     * @dev Deployer setup based on `ETH_KEYSTORE_ACCOUNT` in `.env`:\n     *      - \"scaffold-eth-default\": Uses Anvil's account #9 (0xa0Ee7A142d267C1f36714E4a8F75612F20a79720), no password prompt\n     *      - \"scaffold-eth-custom\": requires password used while creating keystore\n     *\n     * Note: Must use ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner modifier to:\n     *      - Setup correct `deployer` account and fund it\n     *      - Export contract addresses & ABIs to `nextjs` packages\n     */\n    function run() external ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner {\n        new YourContract(deployer);\n    }\n}\n===Deploy.s.sol===\n//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.19;\n\nimport \"./DeployHelpers.s.sol\";\nimport { DeployYourContract } from \"./DeployYourContract.s.sol\";\n\n/**\n * @notice Main deployment script for all contracts\n * @dev Run this when you want to deploy multiple contracts at once\n *\n * Example: yarn deploy # runs this script(without`--file` flag)\n */\ncontract DeployScript is ScaffoldETHDeploy {\n  function run() external {\n    // Deploys all your contracts sequentially\n    // Add new deployments here when needed\n\n    \n    DeployYourContract deployYourContract = new DeployYourContract();\n    deployYourContract.run();\n\n\n    // Deploy another contract\n    // DeployMyContract myContract = new DeployMyContract();\n    // myContract.run();\n  }\n}","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"a64f2c17-70d8-4ca0-b357-cb4c5d56f45b","uuid":"0aef7823-f44c-456a-9cd7-acd80269c5ed","timestamp":"2026-08-19T18:26:23.671Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"===YourContract.sol===\n//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity >=0.8.0 <0.9.0;\n\n// Useful for debugging. Remove when deploying to a live network.\n\n    console.logString(\"Setting new greeting\");\n    console.logString(_newGreeting);\n\n    greeting = _newGreeting;\n    totalCounter += 1;\n    userGreetingCounter[msg.sender] += 1;\n\n    // msg.value: built-in global variable that represents the amount of ether sent with the transaction\n    if (msg.value > 0) {\n      premium = true;\n    } else {\n      premium = false;\n    }\n\n    // emit: keyword used to trigger an event\n    emit GreetingChange(msg.sender, _newGreeting, msg.value > 0, msg.value);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Function that allows the owner to withdraw all the Ether in the contract\n   * The function can only be called by the owner of the contract as defined by the isOwner modifier\n   */\n  function withdraw() public isOwner {\n    (bool success,) = owner.call{ value: address(this).balance }(\"\");\n    require(success, \"Failed to send Ether\");\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Function that allows the contract to receive ETH\n   */\n  receive() external payable { }\n}\n===DeployYourContract===\n// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.19;\n\nimport \"./DeployHelpers.s.sol\";\nimport \"../contracts/YourContract.sol\";\n\n/**\n * @notice Deploy script for YourContract contract\n * @dev Inherits ScaffoldETHDeploy which:\n *      - Includes forge-std/Script.sol for deployment\n *      - Includes ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner modifier\n *      - Provides `deployer` variable\n * Example:\n * yarn deploy --file DeployYourContract.s.sol  # local anvil chain\n * yarn deploy --file DeployYourContract.s.sol --network optimism # live network (requires keystore)\n */\ncontract DeployYourContract is ScaffoldETHDeploy {\n    /**\n     * @dev Deployer setup based on `ETH_KEYSTORE_ACCOUNT` in `.env`:\n     *      - \"scaffold-eth-default\": Uses Anvil's account #9 (0xa0Ee7A142d267C1f36714E4a8F75612F20a79720), no password prompt\n     *      - \"scaffold-eth-custom\": requires password used while creating keystore\n     *\n     * Note: Must use ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner modifier to:\n     *      - Setup correct `deployer` account and fund it\n     *      - Export contract addresses & ABIs to `nextjs` packages\n     */\n    function run() external ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner {\n        new YourContract(deployer);\n    }\n}\n===Deploy.s.sol===\n//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.19;\n\nimport \"./DeployHelpers.s.sol\";\nimport { DeployYourContract } from \"./DeployYourContract.s.sol\";\n\n/**\n * @notice Main deployment script for all contracts\n * @dev Run this when you want to deploy multiple contracts at once\n *\n * Example: yarn deploy # runs this script(without`--file` flag)\n */\ncontract DeployScript is ScaffoldETHDeploy {\n  function run() external {\n    // Deploys all your contracts sequentially\n    // Add new deployments here when needed\n\n    \n    DeployYourContract deployYourContract = new DeployYourContract();\n    deployYourContract.run();\n\n\n    // Deploy another contract\n    // DeployMyContract myContract = new DeployMyContract();\n    // myContract.run();\n  }\n}","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false}}

\"version\": \"0.0.1\",\n  \"type\": \"module\",\n  \"scripts\": {\n    \"account\": \"make account\",\n    \"account:generate\": \"node scripts-js/generateKeystore.js\",\n    \"account:import\": compile\",\n    \"deploy\": \"node scripts-js/parseArgs.js\",\n    \"flatten\": \"make flatten\",\n    \"fork\": \"make fork FORK_URL=${1:-mainnet}\",\n    \"format\": \"make format\",\n    \"postinstall\": \"shx cp -n .env.example .env\",\n    \"lint\": \"make lint\",\n    \"test\": \"forge test\",\n    \"verify\": \"make verify RPC_URL=${1:-localhost}\"\n  },\n  \"dependencies\": {\n    \"dotenv\": \"~16.3.1\",\n    \"envfile\": \"~6.18.0\",\n    \"ethers\": \"~5.7.1\",\n    \"prettier\": \"~2.8.8\",\n    \"qrcode\": \"~1.5.3\",\n    \"toml\": \"~3.0.0\"\n  },\n  \"devDependencies\": {\n    \"shx\": \"^0.3.4\"\n  }\n}\n===foundry.toml===\n[profile.default]\nsrc = 'contracts'\nout = 'out'\nlibs = ['lib', 'node_modules']\nfs_permissions = [{ access = \"read-write\", path = \"./\"}]\nextra_output = [\"evm.bytecode.opcodes\"]\noptimizer = true\noptimizer_runs = 200\n\n\n[rpc_endpoints]\ndefault_network = \"http://127.0.0.1:8545\"\n\nmainnet = \"https://eth-mainnet.alchemyapi.io/v2/${ALCHEMY_API_KEY}\"\nsepolia = \"https://eth-sepolia.g.alchemy.com/v2/${ALCHEMY_API_KEY}\"\narbitrum = \"https://opt-mainnet.g.alchemy.com/v2/${ALCHEMY_API_KEY}\"\noptimismSepolia = \"https://opt-sepolia.g.alchemy.com/v2/${ALCHEMY_API_KEY}\"\npolygon = {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01ESrvYbnXrrnosiypVBekvn","type":"tool_result","content":"===Makefile===\n.PHONY: build deploy generate-abis get-address account chain compile flatten fork format lint test verify\n\nDEPLOY_SCRIPT ?= script/Deploy.s.sol\n\n# setup wallet for anvil\nsetup-anvil-wallet:\n\tshx rm ~/.foundry/keystores/scaffold-eth-default 2>/dev/null; \tshx rm -rf broadcast/Deploy.s.sol/31337\n\tcast wallet import --private-key 0x2a871d0798f97d79848a013d4936a73bf4cc922c825d33c1cf7073dff6d409c6 --unsafe-password 'localhost' scaffold-eth-default\n\n# Start local chain\nchain: setup-anvil-wallet\n\tanvil\n\n# Start a fork\nfork: setup-anvil-wallet\n\tanvil --fork-url ${FORK_URL} --chain-id 31337\n\n# Deploy the contracts\ndeploy:\n\t@if [ ! -f \"$(DEPLOY_SCRIPT)\" ]; then \t\techo \"Error: Deploy script '$(DEPLOY_SCRIPT)' not found\"; \t\texit 1; \tfi\n\t@if [ \"$(RPC_URL)\" = \"localhost\" ]; then \t\tif [ \"$(ETH_KEYSTORE_ACCOUNT)\" = \"scaffold-eth-default\" ]; then \t\t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url localhost --password localhost --broadcast --ffi; \t\telse \t\t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url localhost --broadcast --ffi; \t\tfi \telse \t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url $(RPC_URL) --broadcast --ffi; \tfi\n\n# Deploy and generate ABIs\ndeploy-and-generate-abis: deploy generate-abis \n\n# Generate TypeScript ABIs\ngenerate-abis:\n\tnode scripts-js/generateTsAbis.js\n\n# List account\naccount:\n===env.example===\n# Template for foundry environment variables.\n\n# For local development, copy this file, rename it to .env, and fill in the values.\n\n# We provide default values so developers can start prototyping out of the box,\n# but we recommend getting your own API Keys for Production Apps.\n\n# DEPLOYER_PRIVATE_KEY is used while deploying contract.\n# On anvil chain the value of it can be empty since we use the prefunded account\n# which comes with anvil chain to deploy contract.\n# NOTE: You don't need to manually change the value of DEPLOYER_PRIVATE_KEY, it should\n# be auto filled when run `yarn generate`.\n\n# Alchemy rpc URL is used while deploying the contracts to some testnets/mainnets, checkout `foundry.toml` for it's use.\nALCHEMY_API_KEY=IZYEU2cWBgnFmgiTAgpWD\n# Etherscan API key is used to verify the contract on etherscan.\nETHERSCAN_API_KEY=DNXJA8RX2Q3VZ4URQIWP7Z68CJXQZSC6AW\n# Default account for localhost / use \"scaffold-eth-custom\" if you wish to use a generated account or imported account\nLOCALHOST_KEYSTORE_ACCOUNT=scaffold-eth-default\n===deployedContracts===\n/**\n * This file is autogenerated by Scaffold-ETH.\n * You should not edit it manually or your changes might be overwritten.\n */\nimport { GenericContractsDeclaration } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth/contract\";\n\nconst deployedContracts = {} as const;\n\nexport default deployedContracts satisfies GenericContractsDeclaration;\n===nextjs package.json===\n{\n  \"name\": \"@se-2/nextjs\",\n  \"version\": \"0.1.0\",\n  \"private\": true,\n  \"scripts\": {\n    \"build\": \"yarn lint && next build\",\n    \"check-types\": \"tsc --noEmit --incremental\",\n    \"dev\": \"next dev\",\n    \"format\": \"prettier --write . '!(node_modules|.next)/**/*'\",\n    \"ipfs\": \"NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true yarn build && yarn bgipfs upload config init -u https://upload.bgipfs.com && CID=$(yarn bgipfs upload out | grep -o 'CID: [^ ]*' | cut -d' ' -f2) && [ ! -z \\\"$CID\\\" ] && echo '🚀 Upload complete! Your site is now available at: https://community.bgipfs.com/ipfs/'$CID || echo '❌ Upload failed'\",\n    \"lint\": \"yarn eslint .\",\n    \"serve\": \"next start\",\n    \"start\": \"next dev\",\n    \"vercel\": \"vercel --build-env YARN_ENABLE_IMMUTABLE_INSTALLS=false --build-env ENABLE_EXPERIMENTAL_COREPACK=1 --build-env VERCEL_TELEMETRY_DISABLED=1\",\n    \"vercel:login\": \"vercel login\",\n {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeCaXyb4sS3JSe5riiMJS","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01CgVQ7HkiHTANtTVUq179fN","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-08-19T182600Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol","content":"//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity >=0.8.0 <0.9.0;\n\nimport \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol\";\nimport \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol\";\n\n/**\n * A USDC tip jar. Anyone can send a USDC tip with an optional message; every tip\n * is recorded on-chain so the frontend can render a public feed. The owner can\n * withdraw the accumulated tips.\n *\n * The USDC token address is supplied at deploy time so the same contract works on\n * Base (0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913) and on a local Base fork.\n * @author BuidlGuidl\n */\ncontract TipJar {\n    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;\n\n    struct Tip {\n        address from;\n        uint256 amount;\n        string message;\n        uint256 timestamp;\n    }\n\n    IERC20 public immutable usdc;\n    address public immutable owner;\n\n    Tip[] public tips;\n    uint256 public totalTipped;\n    mapping(address => uint256) public tippedBy;\n\n    event NewTip(address indexed from, uint256 amount, string message, uint256 timestamp);\n    event Withdraw(address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n\n    error NotOwner();\n    error ZeroAmount();\n    error NothingToWithdraw();\n\n    constructor(IERC20 _usdc, address _owner) {\n        usdc = _usdc;\n        owner = _owner;\n    }\n\n    modifier isOwner() {\n        if (msg.sender != owner) revert NotOwner();\n        _;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Send a USDC tip with an optional message. The sender must have approved this\n     * contract to spend `amount` of USDC first.\n     * @param amount USDC amount in the token's smallest unit (6 decimals)\n     * @param message optional public message shown in the feed\n     */\n    function tip(uint256 amount, string calldata message) external {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n        // Pull the USDC before recording so a failed transfer reverts the whole tip.\n        usdc.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n\n        tips.push(Tip({ from: msg.sender, amount: amount, message: message, timestamp: block.timestamp }));\n        totalTipped += amount;\n        tippedBy[msg.sender] += amount;\n\n        emit NewTip(msg.sender, amount, message, block.timestamp);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Withdraw the full USDC balance held by the jar to the owner.\n     */\n    function withdraw() external isOwner {\n        uint256 balance = usdc.balanceOf(address(this));\n        if (balance == 0) revert NothingToWithdraw();\n\n        usdc.safeTransfer(owner, balance);\n        emit Withdraw(owner, balance);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Number of tips recorded, so callers can page through `tips`.\n     */\n    function tipsCount() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return tips.length;\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":472,"cache_read_input_tokens":35662,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":472},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"a64f2c17-70d8-4ca0-b357-cb4c5d56f45b","uuid":"06ad3c25-c85e-470c-9050-3eac83d8c247","timestamp":"2026-08-19T18:27:48.881Z","request_id":"req_011CeCaXxnhC1rCCQ1nS2gGS"}

Anyone can send a USDC tip with an optional message; every tip\n * is recorded on-chain so the frontend can render a public feed. The owner can\n * withdraw the accumulated tips.\n *\n * The USDC token address is supplied at deploy time so the same contract works on\n * Base (0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913) and on a local Base fork.\n * @author BuidlGuidl\n */\ncontract TipJar {\n    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;\n\n    struct Tip {\n        address from;\n        uint256 amount;\n        string message;\n        uint256 timestamp;\n    }\n\n    IERC20 public immutable usdc;\n    address public immutable owner;\n\n    Tip[] public tips;\n    uint256 public totalTipped;\n    mapping(address => uint256) public tippedBy;\n\n    event NewTip(address indexed from, uint256 amount, string message, uint256 timestamp);\n    event Withdraw(address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n\n    error NotOwner();\n    error ZeroAmount();\n    error NothingToWithdraw();\n\n    constructor(IERC20 _usdc, address _owner) {\n        usdc = _usdc;\n        owner = _owner;\n    }\n\n    modifier isOwner() {\n        if (msg.sender != owner) revert NotOwner();\n        _;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Send a USDC tip with an optional message. The sender must have approved this\n     * contract to spend `amount` of USDC first.\n     * @param amount USDC amount in the token's smallest unit (6 decimals)\n     * @param message optional public message shown in the feed\n     */\n    function tip(uint256 amount, string calldata message) external {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n        // Pull the USDC before recording so a failed transfer reverts the whole tip.\n        usdc.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n\n        tips.push(Tip({ from: msg.sender, amount: amount, message: message, timestamp: block.timestamp }));\n        totalTipped += amount;\n        tippedBy[msg.sender] += amount;\n\n        emit NewTip(msg.sender, amount, message, block.timestamp);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Withdraw the full USDC balance held by the jar to the owner.\n     */\n    function withdraw() external isOwner {\n        uint256 balance = usdc.balanceOf(address(this));\n        if (balance == 0) revert NothingToWithdraw();\n\n        usdc.safeTransfer(owner, balance);\n        emit Withdraw(owner, balance);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Number of tips recorded, so callers can page through `tips`.\n     */\n    function tipsCount() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return tips.length;\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeCaaVaRJ3LXQBweEFWbn","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01WFPGkbwhP3T5KvuRA72jDk","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-08-19T182600Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/packages/foundry/script/DeployTipJar.s.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.19;\n\nimport \"./DeployHelpers.s.sol\";\nimport \"../contracts/TipJar.sol\";\n\n/**\n * @notice Deploy script for the TipJar contract\n * @dev The USDC address is the canonical Base USDC. Because `yarn fork --network base`\n *      runs a local copy of Base at chain id 31337, that same address resolves to the\n *      real USDC token on the fork, so demo tips move real USDC with no real money at risk.\n *\n * Example:\n * yarn deploy --file DeployTipJar.s.sol            # local anvil / Base fork\n * yarn deploy --file DeployTipJar.s.sol --network base   # real Base (requires keystore)\n */\ncontract DeployTipJar is ScaffoldETHDeploy {\n    // Canonical USDC on Base (and therefore on a Base fork).\n    address constant BASE_USDC = 0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913;\n\n    function run() external ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner {\n        new TipJar(IERC20(BASE_USDC), deployer);\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2852,"cache_read_input_tokens":36134,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2852},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"a64f2c17-70d8-4ca0-b357-cb4c5d56f45b","uuid":"75ba26dc-5085-45cf-965b-a14d784c2edd","timestamp":"2026-08-19T18:27:57.337Z","request_id":"req_011CeCaaUcNwbkvBH84Xvrvv"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeCacXkhA7EjerXJWcL54","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01XJQ2k3dwrwY8W23XcVrhfE","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-08-19T182600Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/packages/foundry/test/TipJar.t.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: UNLICENSED\npragma solidity ^0.8.13;\n\nimport \"forge-std/Test.sol\";\nimport \"../contracts/TipJar.sol\";\nimport \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/ERC20.sol\";\n\ncontract MockUSDC is ERC20 {\n    constructor() ERC20(\"USD Coin\", \"USDC\") {}\n\n    function decimals() public pure override returns (uint8) {\n        return 6;\n    }\n\n    function mint(address to, uint256 amount) external {\n        _mint(to, amount);\n    }\n}\n\ncontract TipJarTest is Test {\n    TipJar public tipJar;\n    MockUSDC public usdc;\n\n    address owner = vm.addr(1);\n    address alice = vm.addr(2);\n\n    function setUp() public {\n        usdc = new MockUSDC();\n        tipJar = new TipJar(usdc, owner);\n        usdc.mint(alice, 1_000e6);\n    }\n\n    function testTipRecordsAndTransfers() public {\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        usdc.approve(address(tipJar), 10e6);\n        tipJar.tip(10e6, \"gm\");\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(address(tipJar)), 10e6);\n        assertEq(tipJar.totalTipped(), 10e6);\n        assertEq(tipJar.tippedBy(alice), 10e6);\n        assertEq(tipJar.tipsCount(), 1);\n\n        (address from, uint256 amount, string memory message,) = tipJar.tips(0);\n        assertEq(from, alice);\n        assertEq(amount, 10e6);\n        assertEq(message, \"gm\");\n    }\n\n    function testTipZeroReverts() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.ZeroAmount.selector);\n        tipJar.tip(0, \"nope\");\n    }\n\n    function testOnlyOwnerWithdraws() public {\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        usdc.approve(address(tipJar), 10e6);\n        tipJar.tip(10e6, \"gm\");\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.NotOwner.selector);\n        tipJar.withdraw();\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        tipJar.withdraw();\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(owner), 10e6);\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(address(tipJar)), 0);\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":179,"cache_read_input_tokens":41426,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":179},"output_tokens":20,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"a64f2c17-70d8-4ca0-b357-cb4c5d56f45b","uuid":"ec78de84-3737-432f-bcf2-f9913464c182","timestamp":"2026-08-19T18:28:27.136Z","request_id":"req_011CeCacVp7Vejs8YCUoPBtS"}

public usdc;\n\n    address owner = vm.addr(1);\n    address alice = vm.addr(2);\n\n    function setUp() public {\n        usdc = new MockUSDC();\n        tipJar = new TipJar(usdc, owner);\n        usdc.mint(alice, 1_000e6);\n    }\n\n    function testTipRecordsAndTransfers() public {\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        usdc.approve(address(tipJar), 10e6);\n        tipJar.tip(10e6, }\n\n    function testOnlyOwnerWithdraws() public {\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        usdc.approve(address(tipJar), 10e6);\n        tipJar.tip(10e6, \"gm\");\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.NotOwner.selector);\n        tipJar.withdraw();\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        tipJar.withdraw();\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(owner), {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeCaf449NXyUNJ6F3UZC4","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01C5skruY55KvJLm3EnEnQhF","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-08-19T182600Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/packages/nextjs/app/_components/TipForm.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useState } from \"react\";\nimport { formatUnits, parseUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { useAccount } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { useDeployedContractInfo, useScaffoldReadContract, useScaffoldWriteContract } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\n\nconst USDC_DECIMALS = 6;\n\nexport const TipForm = () => {\n  const { address: connectedAddress } = useAccount();\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [message, setMessage] = useState(\"\");\n\n  const { data: tipJar } = useDeployedContractInfo({ contractName: \"TipJar\" });\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeUsdc, isMining: isApproving } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"USDC\" });\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeTipJar, isMining: isTipping } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"TipJar\" });\n\n  const { data: usdcBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [connectedAddress],\n  });\n\n  const { data: allowance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: [connectedAddress, tipJar?.address],\n  });\n\n  // parseUnits throws on malformed input, so guard the parse.\n  let parsedAmount: bigint | undefined;\n  try {\n    parsedAmount = amount ? parseUnits(amount, USDC_DECIMALS) : undefined;\n  } catch {\n    parsedAmount = undefined;\n  }\n\n  const needsApproval =\n    parsedAmount !== undefined && parsedAmount > 0n && (allowance === undefined || allowance < parsedAmount);\n\n  const handleApprove = async () => {\n    if (!parsedAmount || !tipJar?.address) return;\n    await writeUsdc({ functionName: \"approve\", args: [tipJar.address, parsedAmount] });\n  };\n\n  const handleTip = async () => {\n    if (!parsedAmount) return;\n    await writeTipJar({ functionName: \"tip\", args: [parsedAmount, message] });\n    setAmount(\"\");\n    setMessage(\"\");\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-xl w-full max-w-md\">\n      <div className=\"card-body\">\n        <h2 className=\"card-title\">Send a tip</h2>\n        <p className=\"text-sm opacity-70 -mt-2\">\n          Your USDC balance:{\" \"}\n          <span className=\"font-semibold\">\n            {usdcBalance !== undefined ? formatUnits(usdcBalance, USDC_DECIMALS) : \"…\"} USDC\n          </span>\n        </p>\n\n        <label className=\"form-control w-full\">\n          <span className=\"label-text mb-1\">Amount (USDC)</span>\n          <input\n            type=\"number\"\n            min=\"0\"\n            step=\"0.01\"\n            placeholder=\"1.00\"\n            className=\"input input-bordered w-full\"\n            value={amount}\n            onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n          />\n        </label>\n\n        <label className=\"form-control w-full\">\n          <span className=\"label-text mb-1\">Message (optional)</span>\n          <input\n            type=\"text\"\n            placeholder=\"gm, thanks for building!\"\n            className=\"input input-bordered w-full\"\n            value={message}\n            onChange={e => setMessage(e.target.value)}\n          />\n        </label>\n\n        <div className=\"card-actions mt-2\">\n          {needsApproval ? (\n            <button\n              className=\"btn btn-primary w-full\"\n              disabled={!connectedAddress || !parsedAmount || isApproving}\n              onClick={handleApprove}\n            >\n              {isApproving ? \"Approving…\" : \"Approve USDC\"}\n            </button>\n          ) : (\n            <button\n              className=\"btn btn-primary w-full\"\n              disabled={!connectedAddress || !parsedAmount || isTipping}\n              onClick={handleTip}\n            >\n              {isTipping ? \"Sending…\" : \"Send tip\"}\n            </button>\n          )}\n        </div>\n        {!connectedAddress && <p className=\"text-center text-sm opacity-70\">Connect a wallet to tip.</p>}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":634,"cache_read_input_tokens":44651,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":634},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"a64f2c17-70d8-4ca0-b357-cb4c5d56f45b","uuid":"c805268e-4dd4-40c0-afb8-d0ee2acf09c6","timestamp":"2026-08-19T18:29:23.779Z","request_id":"req_011CeCaf2vBm8nXxnNSsM6QC"}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01SJv4siBahDg72bmt6CLndC","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# 🏗 Scaffold-ETH 2\n2\t\n3\t<h4 align=\"center\">\n4\t  <a href=\"https://docs.scaffoldeth.io\">Documentation</a> |\n5\t  <a href=\"https://scaffoldeth.io\">Website</a>\n6\t</h4>\n7\t\n8\t🧪 An open-source, up-to-date toolkit for building decentralized applications (dapps) on the Ethereum blockchain. It's designed to make it easier for developers to create and deploy smart contracts and build user interfaces that interact with those contracts.\n9\t\n10\t> [!NOTE]\n11\t> 🤖 Scaffold-ETH 2 is AI-ready! It has everything agents need to build on Ethereum. Check `.agents/`, `.claude/`, `.opencode` or `.cursor/` for more info.\n12\t\n13\t⚙️ Built using NextJS, RainbowKit, Foundry, Wagmi, Viem, and Typescript.\n14\t\n15\t- ✅ **Contract Hot Reload**: Your frontend auto-adapts to your smart contract as you edit it.\n16\t- 🪝 **[Custom hooks](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/hooks/)**: Collection of React hooks wrapper around [wagmi](https://wagmi.sh/) to simplify interactions with smart contracts with typescript autocompletion.\n17\t- 🧱 [**Components**](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/components/): Collection of common web3 components to quickly build your frontend.\n18\t- 🔥 **Burner Wallet & Local Faucet**: Quickly test your application with a burner wallet and local faucet.\n19\t- 🔐 **Integration with Wallet Providers**: Connect to different wallet providers and interact with the Ethereum network.\n20\t\n21\t![Debug Contracts tab](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/assets/55535804/b237af0c-5027-4849-a5c1-2e31495cccb1)\n22\t\n23\t## Requirements\n24\t\n25\tBefore you begin, you need to install the following tools:\n26\t\n27\t- [Node (>= v20.18.3)](https://nodejs.org/en/download/)\n28\t- Yarn ([v1](https://classic.yarnpkg.com/en/docs/install/) or [v2+](https://yarnpkg.com/getting-started/install))\n29\t- [Git](https://git-scm.com/downloads)\n30\t\n31\t## Quickstart\n32\t\n33\tTo get started with Scaffold-ETH 2, follow the steps below:\n34\t\n35\t1. Install dependencies if it was skipped in CLI:\n36\t\n37\t```\n38\tcd my-dapp-example\n39\tyarn install\n40\t```\n41\t\n42\t2. Run a local network in the first terminal:\n43\t\n44\t```\n45\tyarn chain\n46\t```\n47\t\n48\tThis command starts a local Ethereum network using Foundry. The network runs on your local machine and can be used for testing and development. You can customize the network configuration in `packages/foundry/foundry.toml`.\n49\t\n50\t3. On a second terminal, deploy the test contract:\n51\t\n52\t```\n53\tyarn deploy\n54\t```\n55\t\n56\tThis command deploys a test smart contract to the local network. The contract is located in `packages/foundry/contracts` and can be modified to suit your needs. The `yarn deploy` command uses the deploy script located in `packages/foundry/script` to deploy the contract to the network. You can also customize the deploy script.\n57\t\n58\t4. On a third terminal, start your NextJS app:\n59\t\n60\t```\n61\tyarn start\n62\t```\n63\t\n64\tVisit your app on: `http://localhost:3000`. You can interact with your smart contract using the `Debug Contracts` page. You can tweak the app config in `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`.\n65\t\n66\tRun smart contract test with `yarn foundry:test`\n67\t\n68\t- Edit your smart contracts in `packages/foundry/contracts`\n69\t- Edit your frontend homepage at `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx`. For guidance on [routing](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/defining-routes) and configuring [pages/layouts](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/pages-and-layouts) checkout the Next.js documentation.\n70\t- Edit your deployment scripts in `packages/foundry/script`\n71\t\n72\t\n73\t## Documentation\n74\t\n75\tVisit our [docs](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io) to learn how to start building with Scaffold-ETH 2.\n76\t\n77\tTo know more about its features, check out our [website](https://scaffoldeth.io).\n78\t\n79\t## Contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2\n80\t\n81\tWe welcome contributions to Scaffold-ETH 2!\n82\t\n83\tPlease see [CONTRIBUTING.MD](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING.md) for more information and guidelines for contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2."}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"a64f2c17-70d8-4ca0-b357-cb4c5d56f45b","uuid":"b5d6514d-26c7-4231-9386-81c42f6b8e03","timestamp":"2026-08-19T18:40:20.797Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-08-19T182600Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/README.md","content":"# 🏗 Scaffold-ETH 2\n\n<h4 align=\"center\">\n  <a href=\"https://docs.scaffoldeth.io\">Documentation</a> |\n  <a href=\"https://scaffoldeth.io\">Website</a>\n</h4>\n\n🧪 An open-source, up-to-date toolkit for building decentralized applications (dapps) on the Ethereum blockchain. It's designed to make it easier for developers to create and deploy smart contracts and build user interfaces that interact with those contracts.\n\n> [!NOTE]\n> 🤖 Scaffold-ETH 2 is AI-ready! It has everything agents need to build on Ethereum. Check `.agents/`, `.claude/`, `.opencode` or `.cursor/` for more info.\n\n⚙️ Built using NextJS, RainbowKit, Foundry, Wagmi, Viem, and Typescript.\n\n- ✅ **Contract Hot Reload**: Your frontend auto-adapts to your smart contract as you edit it.\n- 🪝 **[Custom hooks](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/hooks/)**: Collection of React hooks wrapper around [wagmi](https://wagmi.sh/) to simplify interactions with smart contracts with typescript autocompletion.\n- 🧱 [**Components**](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/components/): Collection of common web3 components to quickly build your frontend.\n- 🔥 **Burner Wallet & Local Faucet**: Quickly test your application with a burner wallet and local faucet.\n- 🔐 **Integration with Wallet Providers**: Connect to different wallet providers and interact with the Ethereum network.\n\n![Debug Contracts tab](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/assets/55535804/b237af0c-5027-4849-a5c1-2e31495cccb1)\n\n## Requirements\n\nBefore you begin, you need to install the following tools:\n\n- [Node (>= v20.18.3)](https://nodejs.org/en/download/)\n- Yarn ([v1](https://classic.yarnpkg.com/en/docs/install/) or [v2+](https://yarnpkg.com/getting-started/install))\n- [Git](https://git-scm.com/downloads)\n\n## Quickstart\n\nTo get started with Scaffold-ETH 2, follow the steps below:\n\n1. Install dependencies if it was skipped in CLI:\n\n```\ncd my-dapp-example\nyarn install\n```\n\n2. Run a local network in the first terminal:\n\n```\nyarn chain\n```\n\nThis command starts a local Ethereum network using Foundry. The network runs on your local machine and can be used for testing and development. You can customize the network configuration in `packages/foundry/foundry.toml`.\n\n3. On a second terminal, deploy the test contract:\n\n```\nyarn deploy\n```\n\nThis command deploys a test smart contract to the local network. The contract is located in `packages/foundry/contracts` and can be modified to suit your needs. The `yarn deploy` command uses the deploy script located in `packages/foundry/script` to deploy the contract to the network. You can also customize the deploy script.\n\n4. On a third terminal, start your NextJS app:\n\n```\nyarn start\n```\n\nVisit your app on: `http://localhost:3000`. You can interact with your smart contract using the `Debug Contracts` page. You can tweak the app config in `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`.\n\nRun smart contract test with `yarn foundry:test`\n\n- Edit your smart contracts in `packages/foundry/contracts`\n- Edit your frontend homepage at `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx`. For guidance on [routing](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/defining-routes) and configuring [pages/layouts](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/pages-and-layouts) checkout the Next.js documentation.\n- Edit your deployment scripts in `packages/foundry/script`\n\n\n## Documentation\n\nVisit our [docs](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io) to learn how to start building with Scaffold-ETH 2.\n\nTo know more about its features, check out our [website](https://scaffoldeth.io).\n\n## Contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2\n\nWe welcome contributions to Scaffold-ETH 2!\n\nPlease see [CONTRIBUTING.MD](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING.md) for more information and guidelines for contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2.","numLines":83,"startLine":1,"totalLines":83}}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeCbXvYd8ceCXLujDeDfr","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Now I'll write the project README with the exact, verified local workflow:"}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3261,"cache_read_input_tokens":87694,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":3261},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"a64f2c17-70d8-4ca0-b357-cb4c5d56f45b","uuid":"cfaa2a6e-9eac-47b2-a108-a21d469ad7bd","timestamp":"2026-08-19T18:40:24.397Z","request_id":"req_011CeCbXuBXB8tcpZLZQkPfP"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeCbXvYd8ceCXLujDeDfr","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01QJKSyQmwGaPzQ6gHNDE6NF","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-08-19T182600Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/README.md","content":"# 💸 USDC Tip Jar (Base)\n\nA Scaffold-ETH 2 dApp: an on-chain **USDC tip jar** for Base plus a page with a\nlive tip feed and a form to send a tip.\n\n- **Contract:** `packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol` — pulls USDC via\n  `transferFrom`, records each tip (`from`, `amount`, `message`, `timestamp`),\n  emits `NewTip`, and lets the owner withdraw. The USDC address is passed at\n  deploy time, wired to Base USDC `0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913`.\n- **Frontend:** `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx` (+ `app/_components/`) — jar stats,\n  the `NewTip` feed, and an approve → tip form.\n\n## Local development against real Base state\n\nThe team develops and demos **locally against a fork of real Base**, so tips move\n**real USDC** between test identities with **no real money at risk** — nothing is\never broadcast to Base. The fork is a local copy at chain id **31337**, and the\nfrontend targets it (`chains.foundry` in `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`).\n\nRun each step in its own terminal from the repo root.\n\n### 1. Install dependencies\n\n```bash\nyarn install\n```\n\n### 2. Start a local Base fork\n\n```bash\nyarn fork\n```\n\nThis runs `anvil --fork-url base --chain-id 31337` (the `base` RPC alias is\ndefined in `packages/foundry/foundry.toml`) and imports the default deployer\nkeystore. The fork exposes the **real** Base state — the real USDC contract,\nbalances, and holders — at `http://127.0.0.1:8545`.\n\n> To fork a different network instead: `FORK_URL=<alias-or-url> yarn fork`.\n\n### 3. Deploy the TipJar to the fork\n\n```bash\nyarn deploy\n```\n\n`DeployTipJar.s.sol` deploys `TipJar` with `usdc = 0x8335…2913` and regenerates\n`packages/nextjs/contracts/deployedContracts.ts` under chain id 31337.\n\n### 4. Fund test identities with real USDC (impersonate a Base whale)\n\nBecause it's a fork, we can move existing USDC out of any holder — no faucet, no\nmock token, no real money spent. These are the default Anvil test accounts:\n\n- Alice `0xf39Fd6e51aad88F6F4ce6aB8827279cffFb92266` (account #0)\n- Bob `0x70997970C51812dc3A010C7d01b50e0d17dc79C8` (account #1)\n\n```bash\nexport RPC=http://127.0.0.1:8545\nexport USDC=0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\n# A large USDC holder on Base (verify it still holds USDC on the block you forked):\nexport WHALE=0xcDAC0d6c6C59727a65F871236188350531885C43\n\ncast rpc anvil_impersonateAccount $WHALE --rpc-url $RPC\ncast rpc anvil_setBalance $WHALE 0xde0b6b3a7640000 --rpc-url $RPC   # 1 ETH for gas\n\n# Send 1000 USDC (6 decimals) to each test identity:\ncast send $USDC \"transfer(address,uint256)\" \\\n  0xf39Fd6e51aad88F6F4ce6aB8827279cffFb92266 1000000000 --from $WHALE --unlocked --rpc-url $RPC\ncast send $USDC \"transfer(address,uint256)\" \\\n  0x70997970C51812dc3A010C7d01b50e0d17dc79C8 1000000000 --from $WHALE --unlocked --rpc-url $RPC\n\ncast rpc anvil_stopImpersonatingAccount $WHALE --rpc-url $RPC\n\n# Verify:\ncast call $USDC \"balanceOf(address)(uint256)\" 0xf39Fd6e51aad88F6F4ce6aB8827279cffFb92266 --rpc-url $RPC\n```\n\n> If that whale no longer holds USDC on the block you forked, pick another holder\n> from a Base explorer, or override balance directly with\n> `cast rpc anvil_setStorageAt` / `anvil_setBalance`.\n\n### 5. Start the frontend\n\n```bash\nyarn start\n```\n\nOpen `http://localhost:3000`. Connect the burner wallet, switch it to a funded\ntest identity (import Anvil account #0's key\n`0xac0974bec39a17e36ba4a6b4d238ff944bacb478cbed5efcae784d7bf4f2ff80` into your\nwallet, or use the built-in burner), then **Approve USDC → Send tip**. The tip\nappears in the feed and the jar balance updates.\n\n### Optional: a continuously advancing clock\n\nAnvil only mines when a transaction arrives, so `block.timestamp` sits frozen\nbetween tips. The feed's timestamps are still correct (each tip stamps the block\nit's mined in), but if you want a live-ticking clock during a demo, enable\ninterval mining:\n\n```bash\ncast rpc anvil_setIntervalMining 1 --rpc-url http://127.0.0.1:8545\n```\n\n### Run the contract tests\n\n```bash\nyarn foundry:test\n```\n\n## Production build & IPFS deploy\n\nThe site ships as a static export on IPFS. See **[DEPLOY.md](./DEPLOY.md)** for the\nexact production build, upload, and post-deploy verification commands.\n\n---\n\nBuilt with 🏗 [Scaffold-ETH 2](https://scaffoldeth.io). See the upstream\n[docs](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io) for framework details.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3261,"cache_read_input_tokens":87694,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":3261},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"a64f2c17-70d8-4ca0-b357-cb4c5d56f45b","uuid":"acaa741d-47f4-41d7-a896-1e99a451c7b3","timestamp":"2026-08-19T18:40:54.630Z","request_id":"req_011CeCbXuBXB8tcpZLZQkPfP"}

The USDC address is passed at\n  deploy time, wired to Base USDC `0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913`.\n- **Frontend:** `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx` (+ `app/_components/`) — jar stats,\n  the `NewTip` feed, and an approve → tip form.\n\n## Local development against real Base state\n\nThe team develops and demos **locally against a fork of real Base**, so tips move\n**real USDC** between test identities with **no real money at risk** — nothing is\never broadcast to Base. The fork is a local copy at chain id **31337**, and the\nfrontend targets it (`chains.foundry` in `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`).\n\nRun each step in its own terminal from the repo root.\n\n### 1. Install dependencies\n\n```bash\nyarn install\n```\n\n### 2. Start a local Base fork\n\n```bash\nyarn fork\n```\n\nThis runs `anvil --fork-url base --chain-id 31337` (the `base` RPC alias is\ndefined in `packages/foundry/foundry.toml`) and imports the default deployer\nkeystore. The fork exposes the **real** Base state — the real USDC contract,\nbalances, and holders — at `http://127.0.0.1:8545`.\n\n> To fork a different network instead: `FORK_URL=<alias-or-url> yarn fork`.\n\n### 3. Deploy the TipJar to the fork\n\n```bash\nyarn deploy\n```\n\n`DeployTipJar.s.sol` deploys `TipJar` with `usdc = 0x8335…2913` and regenerates\n`packages/nextjs/contracts/deployedContracts.ts` under chain id 31337.\n\n### 4. Fund test identities with real USDC (impersonate a Base whale)\n\nBecause it's a fork, we can move existing USDC out of any holder — no faucet, no\nmock token, no real money spent. These are the default Anvil test accounts:\n\n- Alice `0xf39Fd6e51aad88F6F4ce6aB8827279cffFb92266` (account #0)\n- Bob `0x70997970C51812dc3A010C7d01b50e0d17dc79C8` (account #1)\n\n```bash\nexport RPC=http://127.0.0.1:8545\nexport USDC=0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\n# A large USDC holder on Base (verify it still holds USDC on the block you forked):\nexport WHALE=0xcDAC0d6c6C59727a65F871236188350531885C43\n\ncast rpc anvil_impersonateAccount $WHALE --rpc-url $RPC\ncast rpc anvil_setBalance $WHALE 0xde0b6b3a7640000 --rpc-url $RPC   # 1 ETH for gas\n\n# Send 1000 USDC (6 decimals) to each test identity:\ncast send $USDC \"transfer(address,uint256)\" \\\n  0xf39Fd6e51aad88F6F4ce6aB8827279cffFb92266 1000000000 --from $WHALE --unlocked --rpc-url $RPC\ncast send $USDC \"transfer(address,uint256)\" \\\n  0x70997970C51812dc3A010C7d01b50e0d17dc79C8 1000000000 --from $WHALE --unlocked --rpc-url $RPC\n\ncast rpc anvil_stopImpersonatingAccount $WHALE --rpc-url $RPC\n\n# Verify:\ncast call $USDC \"balanceOf(address)(uint256)\" 0xf39Fd6e51aad88F6F4ce6aB8827279cffFb92266 --rpc-url $RPC\n```\n\n> If that whale no longer holds USDC on the block you forked, pick another holder\n> from a Base explorer, or override balance directly with\n> `cast rpc anvil_setStorageAt` / `anvil_setBalance`.\n\n### 5. Start the frontend\n\n```bash\nyarn start\n```\n\nOpen `http://localhost:3000`. Connect the burner wallet, switch it to a funded\ntest identity (import Anvil account #0's key\n`0xac0974bec39a17e36ba4a6b4d238ff944bacb478cbed5efcae784d7bf4f2ff80` into your\nwallet, or use the built-in burner), then **Approve USDC → Send tip**. The tip\nappears in the feed and the jar balance updates.\n\n### Optional: a continuously advancing clock\n\nAnvil only mines when a transaction arrives, so `block.timestamp` sits frozen\nbetween tips. The feed's timestamps are still correct (each tip stamps the block\nit's mined in), but if you want a live-ticking clock during a demo, enable\ninterval mining:\n\n```bash\ncast rpc anvil_setIntervalMining 1 --rpc-url http://127.0.0.1:8545\n```\n\n### Run the contract tests\n\n```bash\nyarn foundry:test\n```\n\n## Production build & IPFS deploy\n\nThe site ships as a static export on IPFS. See **[DEPLOY.md](./DEPLOY.md)** for the\nexact production build, upload, and post-deploy verification commands.\n\n---\n\nBuilt with 🏗 [Scaffold-ETH 2](https://scaffoldeth.io). See the upstream\n[docs](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io) for framework details.\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":1,"oldLines":83,"newStart":1,"newLines":120,"lines":["-# 🏗 Scaffold-ETH 2","+# 💸 USDC Tip Jar (Base)"," ","-<h4 align=\"center\">","-  <a href=\"https://docs.scaffoldeth.io\">Documentation</a> |","-  <a href=\"https://scaffoldeth.io\">Website</a>","-</h4>","+A Scaffold-ETH 2 dApp: an on-chain **USDC tip jar** for Base plus a page with a","+live tip feed and a form to send a tip."," ","-🧪 An open-source, up-to-date toolkit for building decentralized applications (dapps) on the Ethereum blockchain. See the upstream","+[docs](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io) for framework details."]}],"originalFile":"# 🏗 Scaffold-ETH 2\n\n<h4 align=\"center\">\n  <a href=\"https://docs.scaffoldeth.io\">Documentation</a> |\n  <a href=\"https://scaffoldeth.io\">Website</a>\n</h4>\n\n🧪 An open-source, up-to-date toolkit for building decentralized applications (dapps) on the Ethereum blockchain. It's designed to make it easier for developers to create and deploy smart contracts and build user interfaces that interact with those contracts.\n\n> [!NOTE]\n> 🤖 Scaffold-ETH 2 is AI-ready! It has everything agents need to build on Ethereum. Check `.agents/`, `.claude/`, `.opencode` or `.cursor/` for more info.\n\n⚙️ Built using NextJS, RainbowKit, Foundry, Wagmi, Viem, and Typescript.\n\n- ✅ **Contract Hot Reload**: Your frontend auto-adapts to your smart contract as you edit it.\n- 🪝 **[Custom hooks](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/hooks/)**: Collection of React hooks wrapper around [wagmi](https://wagmi.sh/) to simplify interactions with smart contracts with typescript autocompletion.\n- 🧱 [**Components**](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/components/): Collection of common web3 components to quickly build your frontend.\n- 🔥 **Burner Wallet & Local Faucet**: Quickly test your application with a burner wallet and local faucet.\n- 🔐 **Integration with Wallet Providers**: Connect to different wallet providers and interact with the Ethereum network.\n\n![Debug Contracts tab](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/assets/55535804/b237af0c-5027-4849-a5c1-2e31495cccb1)\n\n## Requirements\n\nBefore you begin, you need to install the following tools:\n\n- [Node (>= v20.18.3)](https://nodejs.org/en/download/)\n- Yarn ([v1](https://classic.yarnpkg.com/en/docs/install/) or [v2+](https://yarnpkg.com/getting-started/install))\n- [Git](https://git-scm.com/downloads)\n\n## Quickstart\n\nTo get started with Scaffold-ETH 2, follow the steps below:\n\n1. Install dependencies if it was skipped in CLI:\n\n```\ncd my-dapp-example\nyarn install\n```\n\n2. Run a local network in the first terminal:\n\n```\nyarn chain\n```\n\nThis command starts a local Ethereum network using Foundry. The network runs on your local machine and can be used for testing and development. You can customize the network configuration in `packages/foundry/foundry.toml`.\n\n3. On a second terminal, deploy the test contract:\n\n```\nyarn deploy\n```\n\nThis command deploys a test smart contract to the local network. The contract is located in `packages/foundry/contracts` and can be modified to suit your needs. The `yarn deploy` command uses the deploy script located in `packages/foundry/script` to deploy the contract to the network. You can also customize the deploy script.\n\n4. On a third terminal, start your NextJS app:\n\n```\nyarn start\n```\n\nVisit your app on: `http://localhost:3000`. You can interact with your smart contract using the `Debug Contracts` page. You can tweak the app config in `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`.\n\nRun smart contract test with `yarn foundry:test`\n\n- Edit your smart contracts in `packages/foundry/contracts`\n- Edit your frontend homepage at `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx`. For guidance on [routing](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/defining-routes) and configuring [pages/layouts](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/pages-and-layouts) checkout the Next.js documentation.\n- Edit your deployment scripts in `packages/foundry/script`\n\n\n## Documentation\n\nVisit our [docs](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io) to learn how to start building with Scaffold-ETH 2.\n\nTo know more about its features, check out our [website](https://scaffoldeth.io).\n\n## Contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2\n\nWe welcome contributions to Scaffold-ETH 2!\n\nPlease see [CONTRIBUTING.MD](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING.md) for more information and guidelines for contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2.","userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeCbaR3w2KWMd8aRJtTuZ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01ShZjbKxjFMHYQLdMfHX9j6","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-08-19T182600Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/DEPLOY.md","content":"# Deploying the USDC Tip Jar (static IPFS release)\n\nThe site ships as a **static export** hosted on IPFS. This document lists the\nexact build, upload, and post-deploy verification commands.\n\n> Nothing here has been uploaded. Run these when you are ready to publish.\n\n---\n\n## 0. Production build configuration (already in the repo)\n\n- `packages/nextjs/next.config.ts` — when `NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true` it sets\n  `output: \"export\"`, `trailingSlash: true`, and `images.unoptimized: true` so\n  every route emits a static `index.html` and images work off a gateway.\n- `packages/nextjs/utils/scaffold-eth/getMetadata.ts` — Open Graph URL / image\n  and `metadataBase` come from `NEXT_PUBLIC_PRODUCTION_URL` (so they resolve to\n  the production origin, not `localhost`).\n\nTwo changes turn the local (fork) app into the real **Base** production app:\n\n### a. Deploy the contract to real Base\n\n```bash\n# One-time: create/import a funded deployer keystore\nyarn account:import        # or: yarn generate\n\n# Deploy TipJar (usdc = 0x8335…2913) to Base. This regenerates\n# packages/nextjs/contracts/deployedContracts.ts with the 8453 entry.\nyarn deploy --network base\n\n# Verify the source on Basescan (optional but recommended):\nyarn verify --network base\n```\n\n### b. Point the frontend at Base\n\nIn `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`, change the single target entry:\n\n```diff\n-  targetNetworks: [\n-    chains.foundry\n-  ],\n+  targetNetworks: [\n+    chains.base\n+  ],\n```\n\n(During local development this stays `chains.foundry` — see README.md.)\n\n---\n\n## 1. Build the static export\n\nSet the production origin **before** building so Open Graph URLs/images don't\npoint at localhost, then remove old artifacts and rebuild:\n\n```bash\ncd packages/nextjs\nrm -rf .next out\n\nNEXT_PUBLIC_PRODUCTION_URL=\"https://<your-production-domain>\" \\\n  NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true \\\n  yarn build\n```\n\n> **Node 25 note:** on Node 25 the built-in `localStorage` global can exist\n> without the standard Web Storage methods, crashing libraries during static\n> prerender. If you hit that, prefix the build so the remedy is inherited by the\n> build workers (not just `next.config.ts`):\n>\n> ```bash\n> NODE_OPTIONS=\"--localstorage-file=.node-localstorage\" \\\n>   NEXT_PUBLIC_PRODUCTION_URL=\"https://<your-production-domain>\" \\\n>   NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true \\\n>   yarn build\n> # or, if a library trips on web storage entirely:\n> # NODE_OPTIONS=\"--no-experimental-webstorage\"\n> ```\n\n---\n\n## 2. Verify the export BEFORE uploading\n\n```bash\ncd packages/nextjs\n\n# Every route must have emitted its own directory + index.html:\nls out/index.html out/*/index.html\n#   out/index.html  out/blockexplorer/index.html  out/debug/index.html  ...\n\n# Metadata must carry the production origin (expect your domain, no localhost):\ngrep -o 'https://<your-production-domain>[^\"]*' out/index.html | sort -u\ngrep -c \"localhost:3000\" out/index.html && echo \"LEAK: localhost in output\" || true\n\n# The tip jar page itself is present:\ngrep -o \"USDC Tip Jar\" out/index.html\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3. Upload to IPFS\n\nUsing the bundled `bgipfs` (see `packages/nextjs/package.json` `ipfs` script):\n\n```bash\ncd packages/nextjs\nyarn bgipfs upload config init -u https://upload.bgipfs.com\nyarn bgipfs upload out\n# → prints \"CID: <cid>\"\n```\n\nRecord the returned **CID**.\n\n> Convenience one-shot (rebuilds with `NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true` then uploads):\n> `yarn ipfs`. Prefer the explicit steps above so you also set\n> `NEXT_PUBLIC_PRODUCTION_URL`.\n\n---\n\n## 4. Verify AFTER uploading\n\nLet `CID` be the value from step 3 and `GATEWAY` your gateway host\n(e.g. `community.bgipfs.com` or `ipfs.io`).\n\n```bash\nexport CID=<cid>\nexport GATEWAY=community.bgipfs.com\n\n# 1. An unchanged CID from a previous release means identical bytes were\n#    uploaded — re-check the build/upload target before blaming the gateway.\n\n# 2. Root AND a non-home route must both resolve (200, not 404).\n#    Root success alone does NOT prove exported routes resolve:\ncurl -s -o /dev/null -w \"root:  %{http_code}\\n\" -L \"https://$GATEWAY/ipfs/$CID/\"\ncurl -s -o /dev/null -w \"debug: %{http_code}\\n\" -L \"https://$GATEWAY/ipfs/$CID/debug/\"\n\n# 3. Open Graph image + URL use the production origin:\ncurl -s -L \"https://$GATEWAY/ipfs/$CID/\" | grep -o 'og:[^>]*<your-production-domain>[^\"]*' | sort -u\n```\n\nExpect `200` for both routes and the production origin in the OG tags.\n\n---\n\n## 5. ENS content hash (if serving via `<name>.eth`)\n\nOnly after the deployed CID has been reviewed and approved:\n\n```bash\n# Update the ENS content hash to the new CID (via the ENS app or your tooling),\n# then confirm the resolver and a public gateway serve that CID:\ncurl -s -o /dev/null -w \"%{http_code}\\n\" -L \"https://<name>.eth.limo/debug/\"   # expect 200\n```\n\nDo not claim the release is complete until steps 2–5 pass.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2344,"cache_read_input_tokens":90955,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2344},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"a64f2c17-70d8-4ca0-b357-cb4c5d56f45b","uuid":"d409f674-826e-449b-bedb-c84b4d118b1e","timestamp":"2026-08-19T18:41:30.453Z","request_id":"req_011CeCbaPwTYmXNQyjf3hC9m"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeCbfHh4DsrfGUrXKwa5q","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Everything is in place and verified. Here's what I built and confirmed working.\n\n## What shipped\n\n**Contract** — `packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol`\n- Accepts USDC tips via `transferFrom` (SafeERC20), records each tip (`from`, `amount`, `message`, `timestamp`), emits `NewTip`, and lets the owner `withdraw`. The USDC address is injected at deploy time and wired to Base USDC `0x8335…2913` in `DeployTipJar.s.sol`. 3 Foundry tests pass.\n\n**Frontend** — `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx` + `app/_components/`\n- Jar stats (all-time tips, jar balance), a live `NewTip` feed, and an approve → tip form. USDC is registered in `externalContracts.ts` for both the fork (31337) and Base (8453).\n\n## Two constraints, both satisfied\n\n**1. Local demo against real Base state, no real money** — documented in `README.md` and verified end-to-end:\n- `yarn fork` now forks Base at chain id 31337 (I changed the fork default to the `base` RPC alias, since `yarn fork base` doesn't actually forward the arg to the script — I tested this).\n- Test identities are funded by impersonating a real Base USDC whale and moving **real USDC** — nothing is broadcast. I ran the full flow: fund → approve → tip → feed/balance update all worked on the fork.\n- The frontend stays pointed at the local fork (`chains.foundry`), per the playbook.\n\n**2. Static IPFS build, nothing uploaded** — `DEPLOY.md` has the exact build/upload/verify commands. I ran the production build (`NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true` + production origin) and confirmed the pre-upload checks: every route emitted its own `index.html`, the tip jar page is in `out/index.html`, OG tags carry the production origin, and there's no localhost leak.\n\n## One thing worth flagging\n\nThe **real Base production build** requires two deliberate steps documented in DEPLOY.md, which I could not execute here (they'd broadcast to Base): deploy the contract with `yarn deploy --network base` (this regenerates the 8453 ABI entry), then swap the single `targetNetworks` entry to `chains.base`. I kept that as an explicit one-line swap rather than an env toggle — an env-based ternary widened `targetNetworks[0]` to `base | foundry`, which made SE-2's contract-name types collapse to the intersection (only USDC) and broke the build. The one-line swap is the playbook's intended approach and keeps types honest.\n\nVerified: contract tests, local fork + deploy + real-USDC tip, and the static export. Here's what I built and confirmed working.\n\n## What shipped\n\n**Contract** — `packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol`\n- Accepts USDC tips via `transferFrom` (SafeERC20), records each tip (`from`, `amount`, `message`, `timestamp`), emits `NewTip`, and lets the owner `withdraw`. The USDC address is injected at deploy time and wired to Base USDC `0x8335…2913` in `DeployTipJar.s.sol`. 3 Foundry tests pass.\n\n**Frontend** — `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx` + `app/_components/`\n- Jar stats (all-time tips, jar balance), a live `NewTip` feed, and an approve → tip form. USDC is registered in `externalContracts.ts` for both the fork (31337) and Base (8453).\n\n## Two constraints, both satisfied\n\n**1. Local demo against real Base state, no real money** — documented in `README.md` and verified end-to-end:\n- `yarn fork` now forks Base at chain id 31337 (I changed the fork default to the `base` RPC alias, since `yarn fork base` doesn't actually forward the arg to the script — I tested this).\n- Test identities are funded by impersonating a real Base USDC whale and moving **real USDC** — nothing is broadcast. I ran the full flow: fund → approve → tip → feed/balance update all worked on the fork.\n- The frontend stays pointed at the local fork (`chains.foundry`), per the playbook.\n\n**2. Static IPFS build, nothing uploaded** — `DEPLOY.md` has the exact build/upload/verify commands. I ran the production build (`NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true` + production origin) and confirmed the pre-upload checks: every route emitted its own `index.html`, the tip jar page is in `out/index.html`, OG tags carry the production origin, and there's no localhost leak.\n\n## One thing worth flagging\n\nThe **real Base production build** requires two deliberate steps documented in DEPLOY.md, which I could not execute here (they'd broadcast to Base): deploy the contract with `yarn deploy --network base` (this regenerates the 8453 ABI entry), then swap the single `targetNetworks` entry to `chains.base`. I kept that as an explicit one-line swap rather than an env toggle — an env-based ternary widened `targetNetworks[0]` to `base | foundry`, which made SE-2's contract-name types collapse to the intersection (only USDC) and broke the build. The one-line swap is the playbook's intended approach and keeps types honest.\n\nVerified: contract tests, local fork + deploy + real-USDC tip, and the static export. Not verified (can't, without broadcasting): a real Base deploy and a real gateway upload — those are the documented publish steps in DEPLOY.md.","ttft_ms":2944,"ttft_stream_ms":1290,"time_to_request_ms":40,"type":"result","duration_ms":973404,"uuid":"29b67117-7d04-4c4c-893d-62b95343c1ce"}
